RuntimeConfigBuild build_manual_runtime_config(
    RuntimeConfig base,
    const Stream1ModelConfig& stream1_model,
    std::uint64_t free_before_bytes,
    std::uint64_t gpu_headroom_bytes,
    std::uint32_t stream4_flow_scale_ppm) {
    RuntimeConfigBuild build;
    build.manual_config = true;
    build.config = base;
    build.gpu_headroom_bytes = gpu_headroom_bytes;
    build.gpu_budget_bytes =
        free_before_bytes > build.gpu_headroom_bytes ? free_before_bytes - build.gpu_headroom_bytes : 0ULL;
    build.stream3_ring_slots = required_env_u32("BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS");
    build.config.s<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="294" />hrow std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES must be aligned to BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_ALIGNMENT");
    }
    if (build.config.stream4_trigger_candidates == 0U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES must be nonzero");
    }
    if (build.config.shard_capacity_candidates < logical_shard_size_for(build.config)) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES must be at least LOGICAL_SHARD_SIZE");
    }
    if (build.config.stream4_batch_candidates > build.config.shard_capacity_candidates) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES must not exceed BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES");
    }
    if (build.config.stream4_trigger_candidates > build.config.shard_capacity_candidates) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES must not exceed BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES");
    }
    if (build.config.stream3_batch_candidates > build.config.shard_capacity_candidates) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES must not exceed BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES");
    }
    if (build.config.shard_buffer_count != 2U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT must be 2");
    }
    if (build.config.ring_count == 0U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual RING_COUNT must be nonzero");
    }
    fill_runtime_config_build_estimates(build, stream1_model, stream4_flow_scale_ppm, free_before_bytes);
    return build;
}

} // namespace

std::uint64_t ceil_div_u64(std::uint64_t numerator, std::uint64_t denominator) {
    if (denominator == 0) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("ceil_div denominator is zero");
    }
    return (numerator + denominator - 1ULL) / denominator;
}

std::uint64_t local_frontier_capacity(const RuntimeConfig& config) {
    return ceil_div_u64(aligned_global_beam_width(config), config.world_size);

RuntimeConfigBuild build_manual_runtime_config(
    RuntimeConfig base,
    const Stream1ModelConfig& stream1_model,
    std::uint64_t free_before_bytes,
    std::uint64_t gpu_headroom_bytes,
    std::uint32_t stream4_flow_scale_ppm) {
    RuntimeConfigBuild build;
    build.manual_config = true;
    build.config = base;
    build.gpu_headroom_bytes = gpu_headroom_bytes;
    build.gpu_budget_bytes =
        free_before_bytes > build.gpu_headroom_bytes ? free_before_bytes - build.gpu_headroom_bytes : 0ULL;
    build.stream3_ring_slots = required_env_u32("BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS");
    build.config.s<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="294" />hrow std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES must be aligned to BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_ALIGNMENT");
    }
    if (build.config.stream4_trigger_candidates == 0U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES must be nonzero");
    }
    if (build.config.shard_capacity_candidates < logical_shard_size_for(build.config)) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES must be at least LOGICAL_SHARD_SIZE");
    }
    if (build.config.stream4_batch_candidates > build.config.shard_capacity_candidates) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES must not exceed BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES");
    }
    if (build.config.stream4_trigger_candidates > build.config.shard_capacity_candidates) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES must not exceed BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES");
    }
    if (build.config.stream3_batch_candidates > build.config.shard_capacity_candidates) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES must not exceed BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES");
    }
    if (build.config.shard_buffer_count != 2U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT must be 2");
    }
    if (build.config.ring_count == 0U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("manual RING_COUNT must be nonzero");
    }
    fill_runtime_config_build_estimates(build, stream1_model, stream4_flow_scale_ppm, free_before_bytes);
    return build;
}

} // namespace

std::uint64_t ceil_div_u64(std::uint64_t numerator, std::uint64_t denominator) {
    if (denominator == 0) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("ceil_div denominator is zero");
    }
    return (numerator + denominator - 1ULL) / denominator;
}

# Work With Kaggle

Use this skill whenever the task involves Kaggle notebooks, kernels, datasets, or GPU validation runs.

## Project Memory First

For project repositories, read local project guidance before changing notebooks or source:

- `AGENTS.md`
- `memory/PROMPTS.md` and `memory/CHANGELOG.md`, especially recent Kaggle entries
- relevant `test_results/kaggle_*` reports for the same failure/config family

This project has many Kaggle-specific decisions preserved in memory. Do not assume a generic Kaggle workflow is correct when project memory says otherwise.

## Safety And Scope

- Do not upload or publish current uncommitted source to a Kaggle dataset unless the user explicitly approves external export.
- Prefer GitHub-backed Kaggle source when the user has selected GitHub as source of truth.
- Do not push a public Kaggle notebook/kernel unless the user explicitly approves publication or public push.
- Do not make C++/CUDA algorithmic changes while diagnosing Kaggle failures unless the user explicitly approves code changes. Config-only notebook changes are acceptable when useful and consistent with the request.
- Do not revert unrelated local changes. Kaggle notebook edits often coexist with dirty source changes.
- For multi-rank validation, do not fake `WORLD_SIZE=2` on one GPU with both ranks on `LOCAL_RANK=0` unless the user explici<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="692" />ed logs. This rule came from project memory and applies to diagnostic/watch tasks.
- Do not stop production/no-timeout runs unless the user explicitly asks or the notebook itself has a configured timeout.
- If a run appears hung because stdout is redirected, check rank log files and whether live-rank streaming is enabled before assuming a stall.
- Preserve logs even when stopping: terminate gracefully when possible, then download output files.

## Failure Diagnosis Checklist

After a Kaggle failure, collect and scan:

- Kaggle worker status and kernel version
- notebook stdout tail
- rank logs under the notebook output directory
- result JSONL/CSV summaries
- GPU budget/preflight output
- explicit Kaggle messages such as disk quota failures

Common patterns in this project:

- `KernelWorkerStatus.ERROR` plus "more disk space than is available" usually means disk quota/static history arena pressure.
- `return_code=-9` can indicate host RAM OOM.
- `code=3002` / `STREAM_FATAL_STREAM3_DOUBLE_BUFFER_OVERFLOW` points to Stream3/Stream4/threshold/capacity flow diagnostics.
- `final selected count does not match score phase counts` points to final selection/histogram/exact-count mismatch.
- `cudaGraphInstantiate: out of memory` may require config-only headroom/shard-capacity changes before deeper code work.
- Silent output after build may simply mean rank stdout is redirected and live rank logging is disabled.

Write a short report under `test_results/` for meaningful Kaggle validations, sweeps, failures, or launches.

## Response Style

- Be explicit about whether you changed source code, notebook config only, or only launched/monitored a run.
- Include Kaggle kernel slug/version/status when known.
- Include exact local report/log paths in `test_results/`.
- Keep recommendations tied to evidence from logs, not guesses.
- If the next step requires exporting source or publishing publicly, ask for explicit approval first.

# Work With Kaggle

Use this skill whenever the task involves Kaggle notebooks, kernels, datasets, or GPU validation runs.

## Project Memory First

For project repositories, read local project guidance before changing notebooks or source:

- `AGENTS.md`
- `memory/PROMPTS.md` and `memory/CHANGELOG.md`, especially recent Kaggle entries
- relevant `test_results/kaggle_*` reports for the same failure/config family

This project has many Kaggle-specific decisions preserved in memory. Do not assume a generic Kaggle workflow is correct when project memory says otherwise.

## Safety And Scope

- Do not upload or publish current uncommitted source to a Kaggle dataset unless the user explicitly approves external export.
- Prefer GitHub-backed Kaggle source when the user has selected GitHub as source of truth.
- Do not push a public Kaggle notebook/kernel unless the user explicitly approves publication or public push.
- Do not make C++/CUDA algorithmic changes while diagnosing Kaggle failures unless the user explicitly approves code changes. Config-only notebook changes are acceptable when useful and consistent with the request.
- Do not revert unrelated local changes. Kaggle notebook edits often coexist with dirty source changes.
- For multi-rank validation, do not fake `WORLD_SIZE=2` on one GPU with both ranks on `LOCAL_RANK=0` unless the user explici<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="692" />ed logs. This rule came from project memory and applies to diagnostic/watch tasks.
- Do not stop production/no-timeout runs unless the user explicitly asks or the notebook itself has a configured timeout.
- If a run appears hung because stdout is redirected, check rank log files and whether live-rank streaming is enabled before assuming a stall.
- Preserve logs even when stopping: terminate gracefully when possible, then download output files.

## Failure Diagnosis Checklist

After a Kaggle failure, collect and scan:

- Kaggle worker status and kernel version
- notebook stdout tail
- rank logs under the notebook output directory
- result JSONL/CSV summaries
- GPU budget/preflight output
- explicit Kaggle messages such as disk quota failures

Common patterns in this project:

- `KernelWorkerStatus.ERROR` plus "more disk space than is available" usually means disk quota/static history arena pressure.
- `return_code=-9` can indicate host RAM OOM.
- `code=3002` / `STREAM_FATAL_STREAM3_DOUBLE_BUFFER_OVERFLOW` points to Stream3/Stream4/threshold/capacity flow diagnostics.
- `final selected count does not match score phase counts` points to final selection/histogram/exact-count mismatch.
- `cudaGraphInstantiate: out of memory` may require config-only headroom/shard-capacity changes before deeper code work.
- Silent output after build may simply mean rank stdout is redirected and live rank logging is disabled.

Write a short report under `test_results/` for meaningful Kaggle validations, sweeps, failures, or launches.

## Response Style

- Be explicit about whether you changed source code, notebook config only, or only launched/monitored a run.
- Include Kaggle kernel slug/version/status when known.
- Include exact local report/log paths in `test_results/`.
- Keep recommendations tied to evidence from logs, not guesses.
- If the next step requires exporting source or publishing publicly, ask for explicit approval first.

inline Stream1ModelConfig load_stream1_manifest(const std::filesystem::path& dir) {
    const std::filesystem::path manifest_path = dir / "manifest.json";
    const std::string text = read_text_exact(manifest_path);
    const std::string dtype = parse_manifest_string(text, "dtype");
    if (dtype != "fp16" && dtype != "bf16") {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest dtype must be fp16 or bf16: " + manifest_path.string());
    }
    Stream1ModelConfig model;
    model.state_len = parse_manifest_u32(text, "state_len");
    model.num_classes = parse_manifest_u32(text, "num_classes");
    model.hidden1 = parse_manifest_u32_any(text, "hidden1", "hd1");
    model.hidden2 = parse_manifest_u32_any(text, "hidden2", "hd2");
    model.residual_count = parse_manifest_u32_any(text, "residual_count", "nrd");
    model.output_dim = parse_manifest_u32(text, "output_dim");
    model.dtype = dtype == "bf16" ? STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16 : STREAM1_DTYPE_FP16;
    const std::string normalization = parse_manifest_string_default(text, "normalization", "none");
    if (normalization == "layernorm") {
        model.normalization = STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM;
    } else if (normalization == "none" || normalization == "batchnorm_folded") {
        model.normalization = STREAM1_NORM_NONE;
    } else {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest normalization must be none, batchnorm_folded, or layernorm: " + manifest_path.string());
    }
    validate_model_config(model, manifest_path.string());
    return model;
}

inline const char* weight_suffix(const Stream1ModelConfig& model) {
    return model.dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16 ? ".bf16" : ".fp16";
}

inline std::uint64_t fp16_bytes(std::uint64_t count) {
    return count * sizeof(std::uint16_t);
}

    HostWeightBytes weights;

inline std::uint32_t parse_manifest_u32_any(
    const std::string& text,
    const char* primary_key,
    const char* fallback_key) {
    try {
        return parse_manifest_u32(text, primary_key);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {
        return parse_manifest_u32(text, fallback_key);
    }
}

inline std::string parse_manifest_string(const std::string& text, const char* key) {
    const std::string quoted = std::string("\"") + key + "\"";
    std::size_t pos = text.find(quoted);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest missing key: " + std::string(key));
    }
    pos = text.find(':', pos + quoted.size());
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest malformed key: " + std::string(key));
    }
    pos = text.find('"', pos + 1);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest string missing opening quote: " + std::string(key));
    }
    const std::size_t start = pos + 1;
    pos = text.find('"', start);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest string missing closing quote: " + std::string(key));
    }
    return text.substr(start, pos - start);
}

inline std::string parse_manifest_string_default(
    const std::string& text,
    const char* key,
    const std::string& default_value) {
    try {
        return parse_manifest_string(text, key);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {
        return default_value;
    }
}

inline void validate_model_config(const Stream1ModelConfig& model, const std::string& context) {
    if (model.state_len != STATE_LEN) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 state_len must match compile-time STATE_LEN");
    }
    if (model.num_classes < STATE_LEN ||
        (model.output_dim != MOVE_COUNT && model.output_dim != STREAM1_SINGLE_SCORE_OUTPUT_DIM)) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 num_classes/output_dim incompatible with current puzzle");
    }
    if (model.dtype != STREAM1_DTYPE_FP16 && model.dtype != STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 dtype must be fp16 or bf16");
    }
    if (model.normalization != STREAM1_NORM_NONE && model.normalization != STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 normalization must be none or layernorm");
    }
    if (model.hidden1 == 0U || model.hidden2 == 0U || model.residual_count == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden sizes and residual_count must be nonzero");
    }
    if (model.hidden1 < model.hidden2) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden1 must be >= hidden2 because Stream1 reuses hidden1 scratch for residual output");
    }
    if (model.hidden1 % 8U != 0U || model.hidden2 % 8U != 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden sizes must be multiples of 8 for TensorOp path");
    }
}

inline Stream1ModelConfig load_stream1_manifest(const std::filesystem::path& dir) {
    const std::filesystem::path manifest_path = dir / "manifest.json";
    const std::string text = read_text_exact(manifest_path);
    const std::string dtype = parse_manifest_string(text, "dtype");
    if (dtype != "fp16" && dtype != "bf16") {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest dtype must be fp16 or bf16: " + manifest_path.string());
    }
    Stream1ModelConfig model;
    model.state_len = parse_manifest_u32(text, "state_len");
    model.num_classes = parse_manifest_u32(text, "num_classes");
    model.hidden1 = parse_manifest_u32_any(text, "hidden1", "hd1");
    model.hidden2 = parse_manifest_u32_any(text, "hidden2", "hd2");
    model.residual_count = parse_manifest_u32_any(text, "residual_count", "nrd");
    model.output_dim = parse_manifest_u32(text, "output_dim");
    model.dtype = dtype == "bf16" ? STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16 : STREAM1_DTYPE_FP16;
    const std::string normalization = parse_manifest_string_default(text, "normalization", "none");
    if (normalization == "layernorm") {
        model.normalization = STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM;
    } else if (normalization == "none" || normalization == "batchnorm_folded") {
        model.normalization = STREAM1_NORM_NONE;
    } else {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest normalization must be none, batchnorm_folded, or layernorm: " + manifest_path.string());
    }
    validate_model_config(model, manifest_path.string());
    return model;
}

inline const char* weight_suffix(const Stream1ModelConfig& model) {
    return model.dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16 ? ".bf16" : ".fp16";
}

inline std::uint64_t fp16_bytes(std::uint64_t count) {
    return count * sizeof(std::uint16_t);
}

inline HostWeightBytes load_stream1_weights(const std::filesystem::path& dir) {
    HostWeightBytes weights;
    weights.model = load_stream1_manifest(dir);
    const Stream1ModelConfig& model = weights.model;
    const std::string suffix = weight_suffix(model);
    weights.input_weight = read_binary_exact(
        dir / ("input_weight_hxk" + suffix),
        fp16_bytes(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(model.state_len) * model.num_classes * model.hidden1));
    weights.input_bias = read_binary_exact(dir / ("input_bias" + suffix), fp16_bytes(model.hidden1));
    if (model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
        weights.input_ln_gamma = read_binary_exact(dir / ("input_ln_gamma" + suffix), fp16_bytes(model.hidden1));

inline std::uint32_t parse_manifest_u32_any(
    const std::string& text,
    const char* primary_key,
    const char* fallback_key) {
    try {
        return parse_manifest_u32(text, primary_key);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {
        return parse_manifest_u32(text, fallback_key);
    }
}

inline std::string parse_manifest_string(const std::string& text, const char* key) {
    const std::string quoted = std::string("\"") + key + "\"";
    std::size_t pos = text.find(quoted);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest missing key: " + std::string(key));
    }
    pos = text.find(':', pos + quoted.size());
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest malformed key: " + std::string(key));
    }
    pos = text.find('"', pos + 1);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest string missing opening quote: " + std::string(key));
    }
    const std::size_t start = pos + 1;
    pos = text.find('"', start);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest string missing closing quote: " + std::string(key));
    }
    return text.substr(start, pos - start);
}

inline std::string parse_manifest_string_default(
    const std::string& text,
    const char* key,
    const std::string& default_value) {
    try {
        return parse_manifest_string(text, key);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {
        return default_value;
    }
}

inline void validate_model_config(const Stream1ModelConfig& model, const std::string& context) {
    if (model.state_len != STATE_LEN) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 state_len must match compile-time STATE_LEN");
    }
    if (model.num_classes < STATE_LEN ||
        (model.output_dim != MOVE_COUNT && model.output_dim != STREAM1_SINGLE_SCORE_OUTPUT_DIM)) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 num_classes/output_dim incompatible with current puzzle");
    }
    if (model.dtype != STREAM1_DTYPE_FP16 && model.dtype != STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 dtype must be fp16 or bf16");
    }
    if (model.normalization != STREAM1_NORM_NONE && model.normalization != STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 normalization must be none or layernorm");
    }
    if (model.hidden1 == 0U || model.hidden2 == 0U || model.residual_count == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden sizes and residual_count must be nonzero");
    }
    if (model.hidden1 < model.hidden2) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden1 must be >= hidden2 because Stream1 reuses hidden1 scratch for residual output");
    }
    if (model.hidden1 % 8U != 0U || model.hidden2 % 8U != 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden sizes must be multiples of 8 for TensorOp path");
    }
}

[833] assistant: Для IHES получилось:

- `STATE_LEN=72`
- `STATE_ALIGNMENT=16`
- `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80`

То есть физический state выровнен до 80 байт: 72 логических байта + 8 padding. Из этих padding первые 4 байта могут временно использоваться в `FinalResponse` под `target_local_idx`.

[835] assistant: Да по smoke-run это прошло:

- `central_state`: логические 72 байта читаются, padding `72..79` зануляется.
- `generators`: каждый ход хранится как `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80`; первые 72 позиции из IHES perm, padding `72..79` заполняется identity (`p -> p`), чтобы padding не портился.
- `K_1` / solved neighborhood: работает через тот же `MOVE_COUNT=18`, `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80` и те же flat generators, то есть должен строиться в новом layout.

Но: я проверил это только маленьким `depth=2`, `beam=512`, dummy weights. Для полной уверенности по `K_1` надо отдельно прогнать с `BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS=4` и включенным логом, что neighborhood построился без overflow.

Но у нас `test_results` не в git, поэтому если файла нет, вот коротко создать на кластере:

```bash
cat > make_dummy_weights.py <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json
out=Path("dummy_weights"); out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
state_len=72; num_classes=72; h1=64; h2=32; od=1
(out/"manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"state_len":72,"num_classes":72,"hidden1":64,"hidden2":32,"residual_count":1,"output_dim":1,"dtype":"fp16","normalization":"none"}))
def z(n,c): (out/n).write_bytes(b"\0\0"*c)
z("input_weight_hxk.fp16",state_len*num_classes*h1); z("input_bias.fp16",h1)
z("hidden_weight_hxk.fp16",h1*h2); z("hidden_bias.fp16",h2)
z("residual0_fc1_weight_hxk.fp16",h2*h2); z("residual0_fc1_bias.fp16",h2)
z("residual0_fc2_weight_hxk.fp16",h2*h2); z("residual0_fc2_bias.fp16",h2)
z("output_weight_hxk.fp16",h2*od); z("output_bias.fp16",od)
PY
python3 make_dummy_weights.py
```

Создать smoke sbatch:

```bash
cat > ihes_smoke.sh <<'SH'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
#SBATCH -p kaf12
#SBATCH --gres=gpu:1
#SBATCH --time=00:20:00
#SBATCH --job-name=ihes_smoke
#SBATCH --output=ihes-smoke-%j.out

set -euo pipefail

BASE=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
REPO=${BASE}/repo
RUN=${BASE}/ihes_cube
BUILD=${BASE}/build-ihes-${SLURM_JOB_ID}

source ${BASE}/ninja-venv/bin/activate

cmake -S "${REPO}" -B "${BUILD}" -G Ninja \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DCUTLASS_DIR="${CUTLASS_DIR:-${REPO}/external/cutlass}" \
  -DBEAM_PUZZLE_INFO_JSON="${RUN}/puzzle_info.json" \
  -DBEAM_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=80

cmake --build "${BUILD}" --target production_runner -j2

cd "${RUN}"
BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH=puzzle_info.json \
BEAM_WEIGHT_DIR="${RUN}/dummy_weights" \
BEAM_RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE=manual \
BEAM_B_MICRO=64 \
BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY=1 \
BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS=1 \
BEAM_SHARD_COUNT=1 \
BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT=2 \
BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES=2048 \
BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES=2048 \
BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES=2048 \
BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY=0 \
BEAM_FINAL_MATERIALIZE_CHUNK_CANDIDATES=1024 \
BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=268435456 \
"${BUILD}/production_runner" 0 2 512
SH

sed -i 's/\r$//' ihes_smoke.sh
chmod +x ihes_smoke.sh
sbatch ihes_smoke.sh
```

Логи потом:

```bash
tail -f /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube/ihes-smoke-<JOBID>.out
```

Если smoke пройдет, в конце будет примерно:

```text
puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0 ... solution_length=-1
```

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

+# IHES Cube Cluster Smoke
+
+Small MEPhI SLURM smoke test for the Kaggle `cayleypy-ihes-cube` format.
+
+This is not a production solve. It checks that the repository can compile and
+run with:
+
+- `STATE_LEN=72`
+- `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80`
+- `MOVE_COUNT=18`
+- generators loaded from `puzzle_info.json`
+
+The script uses dummy 1-output fp16 weights, depth `2`, and beam `512`, so a
+non-solved result is expected.
+
+## Run
+
+From the cluster work directory:
+
+```bash
+cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/repo
+git fetch origin main --depth 1
+git reset --hard origin/main
+git log -1 --oneline
+
+cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
+cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_smoke/ihes_cube_smoke.sh .
+sed -i 's/\r$//' ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+bash -n ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+chmod +x ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+sbatch -p kaf12 ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+```
+
+Logs:
+
+```bash
+tail -f /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_smoke/logs/ihes-smoke-<JOBID>.out
+```
+
+Expected final line:
+
+```text
+puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0 ... solution_length=-1 solution=
+```
+
+The important checks are in the CMake output:
+
+```text
+Beam state logical bytes: 72
+Beam state physical bytes: 80
+Beam move count: 18
+```

[833] assistant: Для IHES получилось:

- `STATE_LEN=72`
- `STATE_ALIGNMENT=16`
- `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80`

То есть физический state выровнен до 80 байт: 72 логических байта + 8 padding. Из этих padding первые 4 байта могут временно использоваться в `FinalResponse` под `target_local_idx`.

[835] assistant: Да по smoke-run это прошло:

- `central_state`: логические 72 байта читаются, padding `72..79` зануляется.
- `generators`: каждый ход хранится как `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80`; первые 72 позиции из IHES perm, padding `72..79` заполняется identity (`p -> p`), чтобы padding не портился.
- `K_1` / solved neighborhood: работает через тот же `MOVE_COUNT=18`, `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80` и те же flat generators, то есть должен строиться в новом layout.

Но: я проверил это только маленьким `depth=2`, `beam=512`, dummy weights. Для полной уверенности по `K_1` надо отдельно прогнать с `BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS=4` и включенным логом, что neighborhood построился без overflow.

```bash
cat > make_dummy_weights.py <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json
out=Path("dummy_weights"); out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
state_len=72; num_classes=72; h1=64; h2=32; od=1
(out/"manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"state_len":72,"num_classes":72,"hidden1":64,"hidden2":32,"residual_count":1,"output_dim":1,"dtype":"fp16","normalization":"none"}))
def z(n,c): (out/n).write_bytes(b"\0\0"*c)
z("input_weight_hxk.fp16",state_len*num_classes*h1); z("input_bias.fp16",h1)
z("hidden_weight_hxk.fp16",h1*h2); z("hidden_bias.fp16",h2)
z("residual0_fc1_weight_hxk.fp16",h2*h2); z("residual0_fc1_bias.fp16",h2)
z("residual0_fc2_weight_hxk.fp16",h2*h2); z("residual0_fc2_bias.fp16",h2)
z("output_weight_hxk.fp16",h2*od); z("output_bias.fp16",od)
PY
python3 make_dummy_weights.py
```

Создать smoke sbatch:

```bash
cat > ihes_smoke.sh <<'SH'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
#SBATCH -p kaf12
#SBATCH --gres=gpu:1
#SBATCH --time=00:20:00
#SBATCH --job-name=ihes_smoke
#SBATCH --output=ihes-smoke-%j.out

set -euo pipefail

BASE=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
REPO=${BASE}/repo
RUN=${BASE}/ihes_cube
BUILD=${BASE}/build-ihes-${SLURM_JOB_ID}

source ${BASE}/ninja-venv/bin/activate

cmake -S "${REPO}" -B "${BUILD}" -G Ninja \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DCUTLASS_DIR="${CUTLASS_DIR:-${REPO}/external/cutlass}" \
  -DBEAM_PUZZLE_INFO_JSON="${RUN}/puzzle_info.json" \
  -DBEAM_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=80

cmake --build "${BUILD}" --target production_runner -j2

cd "${RUN}"
BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH=puzzle_info.json \
BEAM_WEIGHT_DIR="${RUN}/dummy_weights" \
BEAM_RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE=manual \
BEAM_B_MICRO=64 \
BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY=1 \
BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS=1 \
BEAM_SHARD_COUNT=1 \
BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT=2 \
BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES=2048 \
BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES=2048 \
BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES=2048 \
BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY=0 \
BEAM_FINAL_MATERIALIZE_CHUNK_CANDIDATES=1024 \
BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=268435456 \
"${BUILD}/production_runner" 0 2 512
SH

sed -i 's/\r$//' ihes_smoke.sh
chmod +x ihes_smoke.sh
sbatch ihes_smoke.sh
```

Логи потом:

```

```text
puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0 ... solution_length=-1
```

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

+
+Small MEPhI SLURM smoke test for the Kaggle `cayleypy-ihes-cube` format.

+This is not a production solve. It checks that the repository can compile and

+- `STATE_STORAGE_LEN=80`

+non-solved result is expected.

+

+

+

+puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0 ... solution_length=-1 solution=

+  unzip -o "${DATA_DIR}/cayleypy-ihes-cube.zip" -d "${DATA_DIR}"
+else
+  echo "download_ihes_cube_data=0"
+fi
+
+"${NINJA_VENV_DIR}/bin/python" \
+  "${REPO_DIR}/hpc/ihes_cube_smoke/make_dummy_weights.py" \
+  "${WEIGHT_DIR}"
+
+cmake -S "${REPO_DIR}" -B "${BUILD_DIR}" -G Ninja \
+  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
+  -DCUTLASS_DIR="${CUTLASS_DIR}" \
+  -DNCCL_INCLUDE_DIR="${NCCL_INCLUDE_DIR}" \
+  -DNCCL_LIBRARY="${NCCL_LIBRARY}" \
+  -DBEAM_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=80 \
+  -DBEAM_PUZZLE_INFO_JSON="${DATA_DIR}/puzzle_info.json" \
+  -DBEAM_ENABLE_DEBUG=ON \
+  -DBEAM_ENABLE_DEPTH_LOGS=ON
+
+cmake --build "${BUILD_DIR}" --target production_runner -j "${SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK:-8}"
+
+cd "${RUN_DIR}"
+ln -sfn "${DATA_DIR}" data
+mkdir -p test_results
+
+BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH=data/puzzle_info.json \
+BEAM_WEIGHT_DIR="${WEIGHT_DIR}" \
+BEAM_RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE=manual \
+BEAM_B_MICRO=64 \
+BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY=1 \
+BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS=1 \
+BEAM_SHARD_COUNT=1 \
+BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT=2 \
+BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES=2048 \
+BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES=2048 \
+BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES=2048 \
+BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY=0 \
+BEAM_FINAL_MATERIALIZE_CHUNK_CANDIDATES=1024 \
+BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=268435456 \
+"${BUILD_DIR}/production_runner" 0 2 512
+
+echo "finished_at=$(date -Is)"
*** End Patch

---
name: mephi-hpc-work-rules
description: Use when helping with NRNU MEPhI HPC, ssh mephi, vokirova, basis, kaf12, SLURM sbatch jobs, MEPhI cluster file transfers, software setup, or partition/resource selection.
---

# MEPhI HPC Work Rules

Use this skill before giving or running commands for MEPhI HPC work.

## Operating Identity

- Entity: `mephi_hpc_work_rules`
- Type: `operational_rules`
- State: `active`
- Account login: `vokirova`
- Entry command: `ssh mephi`
- Current priority second hop: `ssh basis`
- Current priority partition: `kaf12`
- Current submit command: `sbatch -p kaf12 start.sh`
- Job data root: `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova`
- User software roots: `/mnt/pool/3/vokirova`, `/mnt/pool/4/vokirova`
- Visible terminal gateway: one user-visible `cmd.exe`, Windows Terminal, or
  equivalent terminal session where the user can see every MEPhI HPC command and
  every output line.

## Non-Negotiable Safety Rules

Never reveal, print, copy into chat, upload, or send:

- the private key file contents;
- the private key passphrase;
- any command output that contains private key material or passphrase text.

The private key path may be referenced as configuration context:

```text
~/.ssh/MEPHI/id_rsa_mephi_hpc
```

The public key `.pub` may be sent only to MEPhI HPC administrators for access
registration or key replacement. Do not dump it into chat unless the user is
explicitly preparing an administrator-facing message and the destination is
clear.

Do not ask the user to type a passphrase into chat. If SSH prompts for a
passphrase, the user handles that prompt locally in the terminal.

## Visible Terminal Gateway Rule

Before doing MEPhI HPC work, create or identify one visible terminal gateway.
This is a terminal window on the user's screen, usually `cmd.exe` or Windows
Terminal, where the session is connected with:

```bash
ssh mephi
ssh basis
``<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1812" />mephi:/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>/slurm-<id>.out <local-path>
```

If syncing large trees to `/mnt/pool/6`, avoid expensive delta scans when
possible. Prefer explicit scoped transfers, and when `rsync` is necessary use
options such as:

```bash
rsync --whole-file --inplace
```

## Response Behavior For Codex

Before proposing or running HPC commands:

1. Check whether a single visible terminal gateway is active and route cluster
   work through it.
2. If the gateway is missing, open/create it first; if its launcher is outdated,
   update the launcher first.
3. If Codex will paste/type commands, verify the target prompt or have the user
   paste the commands manually.
4. Classify the action as setup, transfer, compile, queue inspection, or
   computation.
5. If it is computation, convert it into a SLURM job script and `sbatch`
   submission.
6. Keep every job under `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>`.
7. Select a partition according to the policy above.
8. Avoid stdout/stderr redirection for beginners unless there is a clear reason.
9. Always tell the user the job id and output file path after submission.

If a requested action would violate these rules, refuse that action briefly and
provide the compliant SLURM-based alternative.

## Source Snapshot

Primary sources:

- MEPhI HPC FAQ: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/faq`
- OpenSSH Unix: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/openssh`
- OpenSSH WSL: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/openssh-wsl`
- PuTTY note: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/putty`
- Account request/renewal: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/join`
- HPC usage rules: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/usage`
- HPC resources: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/perfomance`
- User guide PDF: `https://ut.mephi.ru/pdf/projects/hpc/unicluster/userguide.pdf`
- Mailing list: `https://lists.mephi.ru/listinfo/hpc`

[908] assistant: Правила по постановке в очередь такие:

- Все вычисления только через `sbatch`, не запускать расчет напрямую по `ssh`.
- Работать через видимый терминал: `ssh mephi`, потом `ssh basis`.
- Рабочая папка под задачу: `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>`.
- Для текущего approved workflow использовать `basis:kaf12`, команда вида:
  ```bash
  sbatch -p kaf12 start.sh
  ```
- После `sbatch` обязательно зафиксировать job id.
- Статус:
  ```bash
  scontrol show job <id>
  ```
- Логи:
  ```bash
  cat slurm-<id>.out
  ```
  или если в скрипте задан `#SBATCH --output=...`, читать этот конкретный файл.
- Ресурсы:
  ```bash
  sstat -a -j <id>
  ```
- Отмена:
  ```bash
  scancel <id>
  ```
- GPU partition можно использовать только для реального GPU-кода. Наш IHES smoke CUDA/GPU использует, значит `kaf12` ок.
- Не использовать `sudo`, `apt`, `su`; пакеты только в venv/user dirs.
- Важное: если я буду давать команды для кластера, их лучше вставлять в твой видимый терминал на `vokirova@basis`, не через скрытый ssh.

 ```
 
+To avoid cancelling existing jobs, submit after the newest active `vokirova`
+job:
+
+```bash
+DEP_AFTER="$(squeue -h -u vokirova -o '%i' | tail -n 1)"
+if [ -n "${DEP_AFTER}" ]; then
+  sbatch -p kaf12 --dependency=afterany:${DEP_AFTER} ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+else
+  sbatch -p kaf12 ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+fi
+```
+
 Logs:
*** End Patch

---
name: mephi-hpc-work-rules
description: Use when helping with NRNU MEPhI HPC, ssh mephi, vokirova, basis, kaf12, SLURM sbatch jobs, MEPhI cluster file transfers, software setup, or partition/resource selection.
---

# MEPhI HPC Work Rules

Use this skill before giving or running commands for MEPhI HPC work.

## Operating Identity

- Entity: `mephi_hpc_work_rules`
- Type: `operational_rules`
- State: `active`
- Account login: `vokirova`
- Entry command: `ssh mephi`
- Current priority second hop: `ssh basis`
- Current priority partition: `kaf12`
- Current submit command: `sbatch -p kaf12 start.sh`
- Job data root: `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova`
- User software roots: `/mnt/pool/3/vokirova`, `/mnt/pool/4/vokirova`
- Visible terminal gateway: one user-visible `cmd.exe`, Windows Terminal, or
  equivalent terminal session where the user can see every MEPhI HPC command and
  every output line.

## Non-Negotiable Safety Rules

Never reveal, print, copy into chat, upload, or send:

- the private key file contents;
- the private key passphrase;
- any command output that contains private key material or passphrase text.

The private key path may be referenced as configuration context:

```text
~/.ssh/MEPHI/id_rsa_mephi_hpc
```

The public key `.pub` may be sent only to MEPhI HPC administrators for access
registration or key replacement. Do not dump it into chat unless the user is
explicitly preparing an administrator-facing message and the destination is
clear.

Do not ask the user to type a passphrase into chat. If SSH prompts for a
passphrase, the user handles that prompt locally in the terminal.

## Visible Terminal Gateway Rule

Before doing MEPhI HPC work, create or identify one visible terminal gateway.
This is a terminal window on the user's screen, usually `cmd.exe` or Windows
Terminal, where the session is connected with:

```bash
ssh mephi
ssh basis
``<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1812" />mephi:/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>/slurm-<id>.out <local-path>
```

If syncing large trees to `/mnt/pool/6`, avoid expensive delta scans when
possible. Prefer explicit scoped transfers, and when `rsync` is necessary use
options such as:

```bash
rsync --whole-file --inplace
```

## Response Behavior For Codex

Before proposing or running HPC commands:

1. Check whether a single visible terminal gateway is active and route cluster
   work through it.
2. If the gateway is missing, open/create it first; if its launcher is outdated,
   update the launcher first.
3. If Codex will paste/type commands, verify the target prompt or have the user
   paste the commands manually.
4. Classify the action as setup, transfer, compile, queue inspection, or
   computation.
5. If it is computation, convert it into a SLURM job script and `sbatch`
   submission.
6. Keep every job under `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>`.
7. Select a partition according to the policy above.
8. Avoid stdout/stderr redirection for beginners unless there is a clear reason.
9. Always tell the user the job id and output file path after submission.

If a requested action would violate these rules, refuse that action briefly and
provide the compliant SLURM-based alternative.

## Source Snapshot

Primary sources:

- MEPhI HPC FAQ: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/faq`
- OpenSSH Unix: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/openssh`
- OpenSSH WSL: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/openssh-wsl`
- PuTTY note: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/putty`
- Account request/renewal: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/join`
- HPC usage rules: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/usage`
- HPC resources: `https://it.mephi.ru/hpc/perfomance`
- User guide PDF: `https://ut.mephi.ru/pdf/projects/hpc/unicluster/userguide.pdf`
- Mailing list: `https://lists.mephi.ru/listinfo/hpc`

Local reference snapshot:

- `mephi_hpc_work_rules_sources.md`
- `docs/deployment/mephi_hpc_operating_rules.md`
- `docs/contracts/SUMMARY.md`

+To avoid cancelling existing jobs, submit after the newest active `vokirova`
+job:
+
+```bash
+DEP_AFTER="$(squeue -h -u vokirova -o '%i' | tail -n 1)"
+if [ -n "${DEP_AFTER}" ]; then
+  sbatch -p kaf12 --dependency=afterany:${DEP_AFTER} ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+else
+  sbatch -p kaf12 ihes_cube_smoke.sh
+fi
+```
+
